# Nexus 4 — In-situ metrics (no charts). 
# Fold/Clock/Metrics pipeline on two byte streams:
#   A) Concatenated doubles of constants [π, e, φ, πφ, πφ², πe, eφ²] (repeated to length)
#   B) SHA-256 chain seeded from the same constant block (dynamic mixing)
#
# Outputs:
#  - f* index (dominant FFT bin excluding DC), derived period P (samples)
#  - cycle amplitudes (RMS) summary (first 8 cycles)
#  - per-cycle log-decrement δ_n, and mean \bar{δ}
#  - Q_Ψ = π / \bar{δ}  (reported only if \bar{δ} > 0)
#  - 9-sector phase histograms for original / nibble-swap / endian-swap
#  - χ² vs uniform for each histogram (df=8; p-value not computed here)
#
# Fold: radix-shear b -> θ = 2π b / 256; signal s = cos θ
# Clock: dominant non-DC rFFT magnitude
# Basis swaps: nibble-swap; 8-byte endian-swap
#
# Note: No plots; all numeric prints.

import math
import struct
import hashlib
import numpy as np
from collections import Counter

# ---------- Helpers ----------

def doubles_le_bytes(vals):
    """Pack iterable of floats into little-endian IEEE-754 64-bit bytes."""
    out = bytearray()
    for v in vals:
        out.extend(struct.pack('<d', float(v)))
    return bytes(out)

def nibble_swap(bs: bytes) -> bytes:
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8).copy()
    hi = arr >> 4
    lo = arr & 0x0F
    arr_sw = (lo << 4) | hi
    return arr_sw.tobytes()

def endian_swap_8(bs: bytes) -> bytes:
    # swap every 8-byte chunk endianness; leftover tail (if any) left as-is
    out = bytearray()
    for i in range(0, len(bs), 8):
        chunk = bs[i:i+8]
        if len(chunk) == 8:
            out.extend(chunk[::-1])
        else:
            out.extend(chunk)
    return bytes(out)

def fold_radix_shear_cos(bs: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
    # b -> theta; s = cos(theta)
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8)
    theta = (2.0 * math.pi / 256.0) * arr.astype(np.float64)
    return np.cos(theta)

def dominant_bin_excluding_dc(x: np.ndarray):
    # rFFT magnitudes; exclude DC (bin 0)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x - np.mean(x))
    mags = np.abs(X)
    if len(mags) <= 1:
        return 0, mags
    k = 1 + int(np.argmax(mags[1:]))  # index of dominant non-DC bin
    return k, mags

def derive_period_from_bin(N: int, k: int) -> int:
    if k <= 0:
        return 0
    # Period in samples corresponding to DFT bin k over length N: P = N / k
    P = int(round(N / k))
    return max(P, 1)

def cycle_rms(x: np.ndarray, P: int):
    if P <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    n_cycles = len(x) // P
    if n_cycles < 2:
        return np.array([])
    rms = []
    for c in range(n_cycles):
        seg = x[c*P:(c+1)*P]
        rms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(seg*seg))))
    return np.array(rms)

def log_decrements(rms: np.ndarray):
    if len(rms) < 2:
        return np.array([])
    # avoid zero/negatives by tiny epsilon
    eps = 1e-15
    rms = np.maximum(rms, eps)
    return np.log(rms[:-1] / rms[1:])

def phase9_hist(bs: bytes):
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8)
    # angle sector index: floor(9 * b / 256)
    bins = (9 * arr) // 256  # integer 0..8
    counts = np.bincount(bins, minlength=9)
    return counts

def chi2_vs_uniform(counts: np.ndarray):
    total = int(counts.sum())
    if total == 0:
        return 0.0
    expected = total / 9.0
    return float(np.sum((counts - expected)**2 / expected))

def summarize(name, bs: bytes):
    print(f"\n===== {name} =====")
    print(f"bytes: {len(bs):,}")

    # Fold
    s = fold_radix_shear_cos(bs)
    N = len(s)

    # Clock
    k, mags = dominant_bin_excluding_dc(s)
    P = derive_period_from_bin(N, k)
    print(f"dominant_bin k: {k} / rFFT_len: {len(mags)}  -> period P: {P} samples")

    # Amplitudes (first 8 cycles)
    rms = cycle_rms(s, P)
    if len(rms) == 0:
        print("cycles: <insufficient>")
        deltas = np.array([])
    else:
        n_show = min(8, len(rms))
        print(f"cycles: {len(rms)}; RMS first {n_show}: " + ", ".join(f"{r:.6f}" for r in rms[:n_show]))
        deltas = log_decrements(rms)
        if len(deltas) > 0:
            mean_delta = float(np.mean(deltas))
            std_delta = float(np.std(deltas, ddof=1)) if len(deltas) > 1 else 0.0
            print(f"δ_n (first 7): " + ", ".join(f"{d:.6f}" for d in deltas[:7]))
            print(f"mean δ: {mean_delta:.6f}  (std: {std_delta:.6f})")
            if mean_delta > 0:
                Qpsi = math.pi / mean_delta
                print(f"Q_Ψ = π/δ̄ = {Qpsi:.6f}")
        else:
            print("δ_n: <insufficient cycles>")

    # Phase-9 histograms
    h0 = phase9_hist(bs)
    h1 = phase9_hist(nibble_swap(bs))
    h2 = phase9_hist(endian_swap_8(bs))

    chi0 = chi2_vs_uniform(h0)
    chi1 = chi2_vs_uniform(h1)
    chi2 = chi2_vs_uniform(h2)

    def fmt_hist(h): 
        return "[" + ", ".join(str(int(x)) for x in h.tolist()) + "]"

    print(f"phase-9 counts (orig):  {fmt_hist(h0)}  χ²={chi0:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (nibb):  {fmt_hist(h1)}  χ²={chi1:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (end8):  {fmt_hist(h2)}  χ²={chi2:.3f}")


# ---------- Build streams ----------

pi = math.pi
e  = math.e
phi = (1 + math.sqrt(5)) / 2.0

vals = [pi, e, phi, pi*phi, pi*(phi**2), pi*e, e*(phi**2)]

# A) Constant block repeated
block = doubles_le_bytes(vals)
repeatA = 2048  # 7*8*2048 = 114,688 bytes
streamA = block * repeatA

# B) SHA-256 chain seeded from the same block
target_len_B = 131072  # 128 KiB
bufs = []
seed = hashlib.sha256(block).digest()
bufs.append(seed)
while sum(len(x) for x in bufs) < target_len_B:
    seed = hashlib.sha256(seed).digest()
    bufs.append(seed)
streamB = b"".join(bufs)[:target_len_B]

# ---------- Run summaries ----------
summarize("A) CONST-DOUBLES (repeated)", streamA)
summarize("B) SHA-CHAIN (seeded by CONST block)", streamB)

            if mean_delta > 0:

                print(f\"Q_Ψ = π/δ̄ = {Qpsi:.6f}\")

    h0 = phase9_hist(bs)
    h1 = phase9_hist(nibble_swap(bs))
    h2 = phase9_hist(endian_swap_8(bs))

    chi0 = chi2_vs_uniform(h0)
    chi1 = chi2_vs_uniform(h1)
    chi2 = chi2_vs_uniform(h2)

    def fmt_hist(h): 
        return "[" + ", ".join(str(int(x)) for x in h.tolist()) + "]"

    print(f"phase-9 counts (orig):  {fmt_hist(h0)}  χ²={chi0:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (nibb):  {fmt_hist(h1)}  χ²={chi1:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (end8):  {fmt_hist(h2)}  χ²={chi2:.3f}")

## ASSISTANT
# Re-run with corrected string formatting (no backslash escapes inside f-strings).

import math
import struct
import hashlib
import numpy as np

def doubles_le_bytes(vals):
    out = bytearray()
    for v in vals:
        out.extend(struct.pack('<d', float(v)))
    return bytes(out)

def nibble_swap(bs: bytes) -> bytes:
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8).copy()
    hi = arr >> 4
    lo = arr & 0x0F
    arr_sw = (lo << 4) | hi
    return arr_sw.tobytes()

def endian_swap_8(bs: bytes) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    for i in range(0, len(bs), 8):
        chunk = bs[i:i+8]
        if len(chunk) == 8:
            out.extend(chunk[::-1])
        else:
            out.extend(chunk)
    return bytes(out)

def fold_radix_shear_cos(bs: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8)
    theta = (2.0 * math.pi / 256.0) * arr.astype(np.float64)
    return np.cos(theta)

def dominant_bin_excluding_dc(x: np.ndarray):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x - np.mean(x))
    mags = np.abs(X)
    if len(mags) <= 1:
        return 0, mags
    k = 1 + int(np.argmax(mags[1:]))
    return k, mags

def derive_period_from_bin(N: int, k: int) -> int:
    if k <= 0:
        return 0
    P = int(round(N / k))
    return max(P, 1)

def cycle_rms(x: np.ndarray, P: int):
    if P <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    n_cycles = len(x) // P
    if n_cycles < 2:
        return np.array([])
    rms = []
    for c in range(n_cycles):
        seg = x[c*P:(c+1)*P]
        rms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(seg*seg))))
    return np.array(rms)

def log_decrements(rms: np.ndarray):
    if len(rms) < 2:
        return np.array([])
    eps = 1e-15
    rms = np.maximum(rms, eps)
    return np.log(rms[:-1] / rms[1:])

def phase9_hist(bs: bytes):
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8)
    bins = (9 * arr) // 256
    counts = np.bincount(bins, minlength=9)
    return counts

def chi2_vs_uniform(counts: np.ndarray):
    total = int(counts.sum())
    if total == 0:
        return 0.0
    expected = total / 9.0
    return float(np.sum((counts - expected)**2 / expected))

def summarize(name, bs: bytes):
    print(f"\n===== {name} =====")
    print(f"bytes: {len(bs):,}")

    s = fold_radix_shear_cos(bs)
    N = len(s)

    k, mags = dominant_bin_excluding_dc(s)
    P = derive_period_from_bin(N, k)
    print(f"dominant_bin k: {k} / rFFT_len: {len(mags)}  -> period P: {P} samples")

    rms = cycle_rms(s, P)
    if len(rms) == 0:
        print("cycles: <insufficient>")
        deltas = np.array([])
    else:
        n_show = min(8, len(rms))
        print("cycles: {}; RMS first {}: {}".format(
            len(rms), n_show, ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(r) for r in rms[:n_show])
        ))
        deltas = log_decrements(rms)
        if len(deltas) > 0:
            mean_delta = float(np.mean(deltas))
            std_delta = float(np.std(deltas, ddof=1)) if len(deltas) > 1 else 0.0
            print("δ_n (first 7): " + ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(d) for d in deltas[:7]))
            print("mean δ: {:.6f}  (std: {:.6f})".format(mean_delta, std_delta))
            if mean_delta > 0:
                Qpsi = math.pi / mean_delta
                print("Q_Ψ = π/δ̄ = {:.6f}".format(Qpsi))
        else:
            print("δ_n: <insufficient cycles>")

    h0 = phase9_hist(bs)
    h1 = phase9_hist(nibble_swap(bs))
    h2 = phase9_hist(endian_swap_8(bs))

    chi0 = chi2_vs_uniform(h0)
    chi1 = chi2_vs_uniform(h1)
    chi2 = chi2_vs_uniform(h2)

    def fmt_hist(h): 
        return "[" + ", ".join(str(int(x)) for x in h.tolist()) + "]"

    print(f"phase-9 counts (orig):  {fmt_hist(h0)}  χ²={chi0:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (nibb):  {fmt_hist(h1)}  χ²={chi1:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (end8):  {fmt_hist(h2)}  χ²={chi2:.3f}")


# ---------- Build streams ----------

pi = math.pi
e  = math.e
phi = (1 + math.sqrt(5)) / 2.0

vals = [pi, e, phi, pi*phi, pi*(phi**2), pi*e, e*(phi**2)]

# A) Constant block repeated
block = doubles_le_bytes(vals)
repeatA = 2048  # 114,688 bytes
streamA = block * repeatA

# B) SHA-256 chain seeded from the same block
target_len_B = 131072  # 128 KiB
bufs = []
seed = hashlib.sha256(block).digest()
bufs.append(seed)
while sum(len(x) for x in bufs) < target_len_B:
    seed = hashlib.sha256(seed).digest()
    bufs.append(seed)
streamB = b"".join(bufs)[:target_len_B]

# ---------- Run summaries ----------
summarize("A) CONST-DOUBLES (repeated)", streamA)
summarize("B) SHA-CHAIN (seeded by CONST block)", streamB)

## Thoughts
It looks like all bytes mapping to sector 0 is because of multiplication overflow. When using a uint8 datatype in numpy, the calculation doesn’t handle large numbers properly, causing all results to wrap around to 0. I need to adjust the multiplication to use a wider integer type to avoid overflow and get correct sector distribution.

## ASSISTANT
# Fixes:
#  - phase9 histogram now multiplies in int32 to avoid uint8 overflow
#  - clock selection prefers bins whose derived period P is within [8, N/8]; fallback gracefully

import math
import struct
import hashlib
import numpy as np

def doubles_le_bytes(vals):
    out = bytearray()
    for v in vals:
        out.extend(struct.pack('<d', float(v)))
    return bytes(out)

def nibble_swap(bs: bytes) -> bytes:
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8).copy()
    hi = arr >> 4
    lo = arr & 0x0F
    arr_sw = (lo << 4) | hi
    return arr_sw.tobytes()

def endian_swap_8(bs: bytes) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    for i in range(0, len(bs), 8):
        chunk = bs[i:i+8]
        if len(chunk) == 8:
            out.extend(chunk[::-1])
        else:
            out.extend(chunk)
    return bytes(out)

def fold_radix_shear_cos(bs: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8)
    theta = (2.0 * math.pi / 256.0) * arr.astype(np.float64)
    return np.cos(theta)

def choose_bin_with_period_constraints(x: np.ndarray, Pmin=8, Pmax_frac=8):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x - np.mean(x))
    mags = np.abs(X)
    N = len(x)
    # candidate bins exclude DC; sort by magnitude descending
    idx = np.arange(len(mags))
    cand = idx[1:]  # exclude 0
    # sort
    order = cand[np.argsort(mags[cand])[::-1]]
    for k in order:
        P = int(round(N / k)) if k > 0 else 0
        if P >= Pmin and P <= N // Pmax_frac:
            return k, mags, P
    # fallback: original dominant (even if P small)
    if len(mags) > 1:
        k = 1 + int(np.argmax(mags[1:]))
        P = max(int(round(N / k)), 1)
        return k, mags, P
    return 0, mags, 0

def cycle_rms(x: np.ndarray, P: int):
    if P <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    n_cycles = len(x) // P
    if n_cycles < 2:
        return np.array([])
    rms = []
    for c in range(n_cycles):
        seg = x[c*P:(c+1)*P]
        rms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(seg*seg))))
    return np.array(rms)

def log_decrements(rms: np.ndarray):
    if len(rms) < 2:
        return np.array([])
    eps = 1e-15
    rms = np.maximum(rms, eps)
    return np.log(rms[:-1] / rms[1:])

def phase9_hist(bs: bytes):
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8).astype(np.int32)
    bins = (9 * arr) // 256  # 0..8
    counts = np.bincount(bins, minlength=9)
    return counts

def chi2_vs_uniform(counts: np.ndarray):
    total = int(counts.sum())
    if total == 0:
        return 0.0
    expected = total / 9.0
    return float(np.sum((counts - expected)**2 / expected))

def summarize(name, bs: bytes):
    print(f"\n===== {name} =====")
    print(f"bytes: {len(bs):,}")

    s = fold_radix_shear_cos(bs)
    N = len(s)

    k, mags, P = choose_bin_with_period_constraints(s, Pmin=32, Pmax_frac=16)
    print(f"dominant_bin k (constrained): {k} / rFFT_len: {len(mags)}  -> period P: {P} samples")

    rms = cycle_rms(s, P)
    if len(rms) == 0:
        print("cycles: <insufficient>")
        deltas = np.array([])
    else:
        n_show = min(8, len(rms))
        print("cycles: {}; RMS first {}: {}".format(
            len(rms), n_show, ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(r) for r in rms[:n_show])
        ))
        deltas = log_decrements(rms)
        if len(deltas) > 0:
            mean_delta = float(np.mean(deltas))
            std_delta = float(np.std(deltas, ddof=1)) if len(deltas) > 1 else 0.0
            print("δ_n (first 7): " + ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(d) for d in deltas[:7]))
            print("mean δ: {:.6f}  (std: {:.6f})".format(mean_delta, std_delta))
            if mean_delta > 0:
                Qpsi = math.pi / mean_delta
                print("Q_Ψ = π/δ̄ = {:.6f}".format(Qpsi))
        else:
            print("δ_n: <insufficient cycles>")

    h0 = phase9_hist(bs)
    h1 = phase9_hist(nibble_swap(bs))
    h2 = phase9_hist(endian_swap_8(bs))

    chi0 = chi2_vs_uniform(h0)
    chi1 = chi2_vs_uniform(h1)
    chi2 = chi2_vs_uniform(h2)

    def fmt_hist(h): 
        return "[" + ", ".join(str(int(x)) for x in h.tolist()) + "]"

    print(f"phase-9 counts (orig):  {fmt_hist(h0)}  χ²={chi0:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (nibb):  {fmt_hist(h1)}  χ²={chi1:.3f}")
    print(f"phase-9 counts (end8):  {fmt_hist(h2)}  χ²={chi2:.3f}")


# ---------- Build streams ----------

    if len(mags) > 1:

    return 0, mags, 0

def cycle_rms(x: np.ndarray, P: int):

    n_cycles = len(x) // P

    rms = []

def log_decrements(rms: np.ndarray):

def chi2_vs_uniform(counts: np.ndarray):
    total = int(counts.sum())
    if total == 0:
        return 0.0
    expected = total / 9.0
    return float(np.sum((counts - expected)**2 / expected))

def summarize(name, bs: bytes):

    print(f"bytes: {len(bs):,}")
    s = fold_radix_shear_cos(bs)
    N = len(s)

    print(f"dominant_bin k (constrained): {k} / rFFT_len: {len(mags)}  -> period P: {P} samples")
    rms = cycle_rms(s, P)
    if len(rms) == 0:
        print("cycles: <insufficient>")
        deltas = np.array([])
    else:
        n_show = min(8, len(rms))
        print("cycles: {}; RMS first {}: {}".format(
            len(rms), n_show, ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(r) for r in rms[:n_show])
        ))
        deltas = log_decrements(rms)
        if len(deltas) > 0:
            mean_delta = float(np.mean(deltas))
            std_delta = float(np.std(deltas, ddof=1)) if len(deltas) > 1 else 0.0
            print("δ_n (first 7): " + ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(d) for d in deltas[:7]))
            print("mean δ: {:.6f}  (std: {:.6f})".format(mean_delta, std_delta))
            if mean_delta > 0:

                print("Q_Ψ = π/δ̄ = {:.6f}".format(Qpsi))

def doubles_le_bytes(vals):

        out.extend(struct.pack('<d', float(v)))
    return bytes(out)

pi = math.pi
e  = math.e
phi = (1 + math.sqrt(5)) / 2.0
vals = [pi, e, phi, pi*phi, pi*(phi**2), pi*e, e*(phi**2)]
block = doubles_le_bytes(vals)

target_len_B = 131072
bufs = []
seed = hashlib.sha256(block).digest()
bufs.append(seed)
while sum(len(x) for x in bufs) < target_len_B:
    seed = hashlib.sha256(seed).digest()
    bufs.append(seed)
streamB = b"".join(bufs)[:target_len_B]

def header_fold(bs: bytes) -> bytes:
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8)
    if len(arr) % 2 == 1:
        arr = arr[:-1]  # drop last to make even length
    a = arr[0::2].astype(np.int16)
    b = arr[1::2].astype(np.int16)
    d = np.abs(b - a).astype(np.uint8)
    s = ((a + b) & 0xFF).astype(np.uint8)
    # Interleave (d, s) back
    out = np.empty(a.size + b.size, dtype=np.uint8)
    out[0::2] = d
    out[1::2] = s
    return out.tobytes()

def fold_radix_shear_cos(bs: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
    arr = np.frombuffer(bs, dtype=np.uint8)
    theta = (2.0 * math.pi / 256.0) * arr.astype(np.float64)
    return np.cos(theta)

def choose_bin_with_period_constraints(x: np.ndarray, Pmin=8, Pmax_frac=8):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x - np.mean(x))
    mags = np.abs(X)
    N = len(x)
    idx = np.arange(len(mags))
    cand = idx[1:]
    order = cand[np.argsort(mags[cand])[::-1]]
    for k in order:
        P = int(round(N / k)) if k > 0 else 0
        if P >= Pmin and P <= N // Pmax_frac:
            return k, mags, P
    if len(mags) > 1:
        k = 1 + int(np.argmax(mags[1:]))
        P = max(int(round(N / k)), 1)
        return k, mags, P
    return 0, mags, 0

def cycle_rms(x: np.ndarray, P: int):
    if P <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    n_cycles = len(x) // P
    if n_cycles < 2:
        return np.array([])
    rms = []
    for c in range(n_cycles):
        seg = x[c*P:(c+1)*P]
        rms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(seg*seg))))
    return np.array(rms)

def log_decrements(rms: np.ndarray):
    if len(rms) < 2:
        return np.array([])
    eps = 1e-15
    rms = np.maximum(rms, eps)
    return np.log(rms[:-1] / rms[1:])

def summarize(name, bs: bytes):
    print(f"\n===== {name} =====")
    print(f"bytes: {len(bs):,}")
    s = fold_radix_shear_cos(bs)
    N = len(s)
    k, mags, P = choose_bin_with_period_constraints(s, Pmin=32, Pmax_frac=16)
    print(f"dominant_bin k (constrained): {k} / rFFT_len: {len(mags)}  -> period P: {P} samples")
    rms = cycle_rms(s, P)
    if len(rms) == 0:
        print("cycles: <insufficient>")
    else:
        n_show = min(8, len(rms))
        print("cycles: {}; RMS first {}: {}".format(
            len(rms), n_show, ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(r) for r in rms[:n_show])
        ))
        deltas = log_decrements(rms)
        if len(deltas) > 0:
            mean_delta = float(np.mean(deltas))
            std_delta = float(np.std(deltas, ddof=1)) if len(deltas) > 1 else 0.0
            print("δ_n (first 7): " + ", ".join("{:.6f}".format(d) for d in deltas[:7]))
            print("mean δ: {:.6f}  (std: {:.6f})".format(mean_delta, std_delta))
            if mean_delta > 0:
                Qpsi = math.pi / mean_delta
                print("Q_Ψ = π/δ̄ = {:.6f}".format(Qpsi))
        else:
            print("δ_n: <insufficient cycles>")

# Apply 0,1,2 header-fold iterations
summarize("B0) SHA-CHAIN (raw)", streamB)
streamB1 = header_fold(streamB)
summarize("B1) SHA-CHAIN (header-fold x1)", streamB1)
streamB2 = header_fold(streamB1)
summarize("B2) SHA-CHAIN (header-fold x2)", streamB2)

# Nexus Mark1 hunt — phase-increment fold, clock via FFT, damping & phase-9 tests
# No plots; numeric ledger only.

import os, math, struct, hashlib
import numpy as np

def le_double_bytes(x: float) -> bytes:
    return struct.pack('<d', float(x))

def build_const_block():
    pi = math.pi
    e = math.e
    phi = (1 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0
    vals = [pi, e, phi, pi*phi, pi*(phi**2), pi*e, e*(phi**2)]
    b = b''.join(le_double_bytes(v) for v in vals)
    return b

def repeat_to_length(b: bytes, L: int) -> bytes:
    if len(b) == 0:
        return b
    reps = (L + len(b) - 1)//len(b)
    out = (b * reps)[:L]
    return out

def sha_chain(seed: bytes, L: int) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    cur = hashlib.sha256(seed).digest()
    while len(out) < L:
        out.extend(cur)
        cur = hashlib.sha256(cur).digest()
    return bytes(out[:L])

def nibble_swap(b: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    return ((b & 0x0F) << 4) | (b >> 4)

def endian_swap_blocks(b: bytes, block=8) -> bytes:
    if len(b) % block != 0:
        # pad with zeros to block alignment (reversible within our test scope, track logical length separately)
        pad = block - (len(b) % block)
        b = b + b'\x00'*pad
    return b''.join(b[i:i+block][::-1] for i in range(0, len(b), block))

def bytes_to_angles(b: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    # map byte to angle in [0, 2π)
    return 2.0 * math.pi * (b.astype(np.float64) / 256.0)

def phase_increment_fold(byte_arr: np.ndarray):
    # angles
    theta = bytes_to_angles(byte_arr)
    # forward difference (phase increment), wrapped to [-π, π)
    dtheta = np.diff(theta)
    # wrap: ((x+π) mod 2π) - π
    dtheta = (dtheta + math.pi) % (2.0*math.pi) - math.pi
    # signal is cos of increment (motion measure)
    s = np.cos(dtheta)
    # also return positive-angle in [0,2π) for phase-9
    dtheta_mod = (dtheta + 2.0*math.pi) % (2.0*math.pi)
    return s, dtheta_mod

def dominant_period_fft(s: np.ndarray, minP=8, maxP=2048):
    N = len(s)
    # detrend minimal: remove mean
    x = s - np.mean(s)
    # FFT real
    spec = np.fft.rfft(x)
    mag = np.abs(spec)
    # ignore k=0 (DC)
    mag[0] = 0.0
    # translate desired period range to k-bin range
    # period P = N/k  =>  k = N/P
    kmin = max(1, int(N / maxP))
    kmax = max(kmin+1, int(N / minP))
    kmax = min(kmax, len(mag)-1)
    if kmax <= kmin:
        kmin, kmax = 1, len(mag)-1
    k = np.argmax(mag[kmin:kmax+1]) + kmin
    P = int(round(N / k)) if k > 0 else 0
    return max(P, 1), k, mag[k]

def per_cycle_rms_and_delta(s: np.ndarray, P: int, max_cycles=4096):
    if P <= 0:
        return [], []
    n_cycles = min(max_cycles, len(s)//P)
    if n_cycles < 2:
        return [], []
    rms = []
    for i in range(n_cycles):
        seg = s[i*P:(i+1)*P]
        if len(seg) == 0:
            break
        rms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(seg*seg))))
    rms = np.array(rms, dtype=np.float64)
    # avoid zeros/negatives
    rms[rms <= 1e-15] = 1e-15
    deltas = np.log(rms[:-1]/rms[1:])
    return rms.tolist(), deltas.tolist()

def phase9_counts_from_angles(angle_mod: np.ndarray):
    # angle_mod in [0,2π)
    u = angle_mod / (2.0*math.pi) # [0,1)
    idx = np.floor(9.0 * u).astype(int)
    idx[idx >= 9] = 8
    counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=9)
    return counts

def chi2_uniform(counts: np.ndarray):
    n = counts.sum()
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, 0.0
    expected = n/9.0
    chi2 = np.sum(((counts - expected)**2)/expected)
    return float(chi2), float(expected)

def run_ledger(name: str, b: bytes):
    # core fold
    arr = np.frombuffer(b, dtype=np.uint8)
    s, dtheta_mod = phase_increment_fold(arr)
    P, k, pk = dominant_period_fft(s, minP=8, maxP=2048)
    rms, deltas = per_cycle_rms_and_delta(s, P, max_cycles=4096)
    bar_delta = float(np.mean(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    std_delta = float(np.std(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    Qpsi = (math.pi / bar_delta) if bar_delta > 0 else float('inf')
    # phase-9 on Δθ
    c0 = phase9_counts_from_angles(dtheta_mod)
    chi0, exp0 = chi2_uniform(c0)
    # basis tests (nibble, endian on raw bytes, then refold)
    nib = nibble_swap(arr)
    s_n, d_n = phase_increment_fold(nib)
    c_n = phase9_counts_from_angles(d_n)
    chi_n, _ = chi2_uniform(c_n)
    end = np.frombuffer(endian_swap_blocks(b, 8), dtype=np.uint8)
    s_e, d_e = phase_increment_fold(end)
    c_e = phase9_counts_from_angles(d_e)
    chi_e, _ = chi2_uniform(c_e)
    # summarize
    out = {
        "name": name,
        "len": len(arr),
        "P": P, "kbin": k, "peak_mag": float(pk),
        "n_cycles": len(rms),
        "bar_delta": bar_delta, "std_delta": std_delta,
        "Qpsi": Qpsi if np.isfinite(Qpsi) else None,
        "phase9_counts": c0.tolist(),
        "phase9_chi2": chi0,
        "phase9_expected": exp0,
        "phase9_chi2_nibble": chi_n,
        "phase9_chi2_endian": chi_e,
        "first8_rms": rms[:8],
        "first8_delta": deltas[:8]
    }
    return out

def synth_damped_osc(L=131072, P=64, true_delta=math.pi/9):
    # generate cos with exact per-cycle decay: A_{n+1} = A_n * exp(-delta)
    cycles = L // P
    A0 = 1.0
    x = np.zeros(cycles*P, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(P, dtype=np.float64)
    for n in range(cycles):
        A = A0 * math.exp(-true_delta * n)
        x[n*P:(n+1)*P] = A * np.cos(2.0*math.pi * (t/P))
    # map to bytes via inverse pipeline (stays numeric here; we’ll evaluate estimator on x directly)
    # For estimator parity, we’ll pass s=x and skip fold; reuse the RMS/delta code
    rms, deltas = per_cycle_rms_and_delta(x, P, max_cycles=4096)
    bar_delta = float(np.mean(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    std_delta = float(np.std(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    return {
        "true_delta": true_delta,
        "P": P, "n_cycles": len(rms),
        "bar_delta": bar_delta, "std_delta": std_delta,
        "first8_rms": rms[:8], "first8_delta": deltas[:8]
    }

# Build streams
L = 131072  # 128 KiB target
const_block = build_const_block()
CONST_REP = repeat_to_length(const_block, L)
SHA_CHAIN = sha_chain(const_block, L)
V = math.pi * ((1 + math.sqrt(5)) / 2.0)**2
V_REP = repeat_to_length(le_double_bytes(V), L)
RAND = os.urandom(L)

# Run ledgers
ledgers = []
for nm, data in [
    ("CONST-DOUBLES(repeat)", CONST_REP),
    ("SHA-CHAIN(iterated 256)", SHA_CHAIN),
    ("V=pi*phi^2 double(repeat)", V_REP),
    ("RAND(os.urandom)", RAND),
]:
    ledgers.append(run_ledger(nm, data))

# Estimator sanity: synthetic damped oscillator with true delta=pi/9
synth = synth_damped_osc(L=L, P=64, true_delta=math.pi/9)

# Print results compactly
def fmt_ledger(LG):
    Qs = "None" if LG["Qpsi"] is None else f"{LG['Qpsi']:.3f}"
    return (
        f"[{LG['name']}] len={LG['len']}, P={LG['P']}, cycles={LG['n_cycles']}\n"
        f"  δ̄={LG['bar_delta']:.6f} (std {LG['std_delta']:.6f}), Qψ={Qs}\n"
        f"  phase9 χ²={LG['phase9_chi2']:.2f} (exp≈{LG['phase9_expected']:.1f}); "
        f"nibble χ²={LG['phase9_chi2_nibble']:.2f}; endian χ²={LG['phase9_chi2_endian']:.2f}\n"
        f"  first RMS: {', '.join(f'{x:.6f}' for x in LG['first8_rms'])}\n"
        f"  first δ:   {', '.join(f'{x:.6f}' for x in LG['first8_delta'])}\n"
    )

print("=== Nexus Mark1 hunt — phase-increment fold ===")
for LG in ledgers:
    print(fmt_ledger(LG))

print("=== Estimator sanity (synthetic) ===")
print(
    f"True δ={synth['true_delta']:.6f} ; "
    f"Est δ̄={synth['bar_delta']:.6f} (std {synth['std_delta']:.6f}); cycles={synth['n_cycles']}; P={synth['P']}"
)
print("first RMS:", ', '.join(f"{x:.6f}" for x in synth['first8_rms']))
print("first δ:", ', '.join(f"{x:.6f}" for x in synth['first8_delta']))

## ASSISTANT
# Patch estimator: restrict deltas to segments where both consecutive RMS are above a relative floor,
# to avoid late-cycle clamp skewing the mean.

import math, numpy as np

def per_cycle_rms_and_delta_filtered(s: np.ndarray, P: int, rel_floor=1e-6, max_cycles=4096):
    if P <= 0:
        return [], []
    n_cycles = min(max_cycles, len(s)//P)
    if n_cycles < 2:
        return [], []
    rms = []
    for i in range(n_cycles):
        seg = s[i*P:(i+1)*P]
        if len(seg) == 0:
            break
        rms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(seg*seg))))
    rms = np.array(rms, dtype=np.float64)
    r0 = rms[0] if rms.size > 0 else 1.0
    floor = max(1e-15, rel_floor * r0)
    # compute deltas only where rms[i] & rms[i+1] above floor
    mask = (rms[:-1] > floor) & (rms[1:] > floor)
    deltas = np.log(np.maximum(rms[:-1], floor) / np.maximum(rms[1:], floor))
    deltas = deltas[mask]
    return rms.tolist(), deltas.tolist()

# Re-evaluate synthetic and one real ledger using filtered deltas
def eval_synth_filtered(L=131072, P=64, true_delta=math.pi/9):
    cycles = L // P
    A0 = 1.0
    x = np.zeros(cycles*P, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(P, dtype=np.float64)
    for n in range(cycles):
        A = A0 * math.exp(-true_delta * n)
        x[n*P:(n+1)*P] = A * np.cos(2.0*math.pi * (t/P))
    rms, deltas = per_cycle_rms_and_delta_filtered(x, P, rel_floor=1e-6, max_cycles=4096)
    bar_delta = float(np.mean(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    std_delta = float(np.std(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    return {"bar_delta": bar_delta, "std_delta": std_delta, "n_deltas": len(deltas), "first8_delta": deltas[:8]}

synth_f = eval_synth_filtered()
synth_f

# Re-run ledgers with filtered deltas

def run_ledger_filtered(name: str, b: bytes):
    arr = np.frombuffer(b, dtype=np.uint8)
    s, dtheta_mod = phase_increment_fold(arr)
    P, k, pk = dominant_period_fft(s, minP=8, maxP=2048)
    rms, deltas = per_cycle_rms_and_delta_filtered(s, P, rel_floor=1e-6, max_cycles=4096)
    bar_delta = float(np.mean(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    std_delta = float(np.std(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    Qpsi = (math.pi / bar_delta) if bar_delta > 0 else None
    c0 = phase9_counts_from_angles(dtheta_mod)
    chi0, exp0 = chi2_uniform(c0)
    nib = nibble_swap(arr)
    _, d_n = phase_increment_fold(nib)
    c_n = phase9_counts_from_angles(d_n)
    chi_n, _ = chi2_uniform(c_n)
    end = np.frombuffer(endian_swap_blocks(b, 8), dtype=np.uint8)
    _, d_e = phase_increment_fold(end)
    c_e = phase9_counts_from_angles(d_e)
    chi_e, _ = chi2_uniform(c_e)
    return {
        "name": name, "len": len(arr), "P": P, "kbin": k, "peak_mag": float(pk),
        "n_deltas": len(deltas), "bar_delta": bar_delta, "std_delta": std_delta,
        "Qpsi": (float(Qpsi) if Qpsi is not None and np.isfinite(Qpsi) else None),
        "phase9_counts": c0.tolist(), "phase9_chi2": float(chi0), "phase9_expected": float(exp0),
        "phase9_chi2_nibble": float(chi_n), "phase9_chi2_endian": float(chi_e),
    }

ledgers_f = []
for nm, data in [
    ("CONST-DOUBLES(repeat)", CONST_REP),
    ("SHA-CHAIN(iterated 256)", SHA_CHAIN),
    ("V=pi*phi^2 double(repeat)", V_REP),
    ("RAND(os.urandom)", RAND),
]:
    ledgers_f.append(run_ledger_filtered(nm, data))

def fmt_ledger_f(LG):
    Qs = "None" if LG["Qpsi"] is None else f"{LG['Qpsi']:.3f}"
    return (
        f"[{LG['name']}] len={LG['len']}, P={LG['P']}\n"
        f"  filtered nΔ={LG['n_deltas']}, δ̄={LG['bar_delta']:.6f} (std {LG['std_delta']:.6f}), Qψ={Qs}\n"
        f"  phase9 χ²={LG['phase9_chi2']:.2f} (exp≈{LG['phase9_expected']:.1f}); "
        f"nibble χ²={LG['phase9_chi2_nibble']:.2f}; endian χ²={LG['phase9_chi2_endian']:.2f}\n"
    )

print("=== Ledgers (filtered deltas) ===")
for LG in ledgers_f:
    print(fmt_ledger_f(LG))

print("=== Synthetic check (filtered) ===")
print(f"δ̄={synth_f['bar_delta']:.6f} (std {synth_f['std_delta']:.6f}), nΔ={synth_f['n_deltas']} ; target π/9={math.pi/9:.6f}")

The filtered δ̄ values for CONST-DOUBLES, SHA-CHAIN, and RANDOM are around 0.00007, with V repeating zero due to the constant pattern's lack of decay. For phase9 χ², CONST-DOUBLES and V show large values due to non-uniform distributions. SHA-CHAIN and RANDOM χ² values are closer to expected, but a large value for V points to strong bias.
The SHA and random streams show no Mark1 lock, with δ near 0.00007, which is good since no false positives appeared. Next, we could explore other folds, clocks, or streams. Without access to BBP digits, we could potentially generate Pi digits offline, but it's a bit tricky. Given the user's uploaded files, we may pivot to using SHA with structured seeds or the byte arrays provided.

# Exploratory (non-reversible) Header fold: (|b-a|, (a+b) mod 256) over adjacent pairs, then same analysis.
import numpy as np, math

def header_fold_bytes(b: bytes) -> bytes:
    arr = np.frombuffer(b, dtype=np.uint8)
    if len(arr) < 2:
        return b
    a = arr[:-1].astype(np.int16)
    c = arr[1:].astype(np.int16)
    da = np.abs(c - a).astype(np.uint8)
    sa = ((a + c) & 0xFF).astype(np.uint8)
    interleaved = np.empty(da.size + sa.size, dtype=np.uint8)
    interleaved[0::2] = da
    interleaved[1::2] = sa
    return interleaved.tobytes()

def run_header_fold_ledger(name: str, b: bytes):
    hb = header_fold_bytes(b)
    arr = np.frombuffer(hb, dtype=np.uint8)
    s, dtheta_mod = phase_increment_fold(arr)
    P, k, pk = dominant_period_fft(s, minP=8, maxP=2048)
    rms, deltas = per_cycle_rms_and_delta_filtered(s, P, rel_floor=1e-6, max_cycles=4096)
    bar_delta = float(np.mean(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    std_delta = float(np.std(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
    Qpsi = (math.pi / bar_delta) if bar_delta > 0 else None
    c0 = phase9_counts_from_angles(dtheta_mod)
    chi0, exp0 = chi2_uniform(c0)
    return {
        "name": f"HEADER({name})", "len": len(arr), "P": P,
        "n_deltas": len(deltas), "bar_delta": bar_delta, "std_delta": std_delta,
        "Qpsi": (float(Qpsi) if Qpsi is not None and np.isfinite(Qpsi) else None),
        "phase9_chi2": float(chi0), "phase9_expected": float(exp0),
    }

HF_SHA = run_header_fold_ledger("SHA-CHAIN", SHA_CHAIN)
HF_CONST = run_header_fold_ledger("CONST-DOUBLES", CONST_REP)
HF_RAND = run_header_fold_ledger("RAND", RAND)

for LG in [HF_SHA, HF_CONST, HF_RAND]:
    Qs = "None" if LG["Qpsi"] is None else f"{LG['Qpsi']:.3f}"
    print(f"[{LG['name']}] len={LG['len']}, P={LG['P']}, nΔ={LG['n_deltas']}, δ̄={LG['bar_delta']:.6f} (std {LG['std_delta']:.6f}), Qψ={Qs}, phase9 χ²={LG['phase9_chi2']:.2f} (exp≈{LG['phase9_expected']:.1f})")

# Multi-scale stride scan on SHA-CHAIN: Δθ with stride s, s=2..12
import numpy as np, math

def phase_increment_fold_stride(byte_arr: np.ndarray, stride: int):
    theta = bytes_to_angles(byte_arr)
    dtheta = theta[stride:] - theta[:-stride]
    dtheta = (dtheta + math.pi) % (2.0*math.pi) - math.pi
    s = np.cos(dtheta)
    dtheta_mod = (dtheta + 2.0*math.pi) % (2.0*math.pi)
    return s, dtheta_mod

def ledger_stride(name: str, b: bytes, smin=2, smax=12):
    arr = np.frombuffer(b, dtype=np.uint8)
    rows = []
    for stride in range(smin, smax+1):
        sig, dmod = phase_increment_fold_stride(arr, stride)
        P, k, pk = dominant_period_fft(sig, minP=8, maxP=4096)
        rms, deltas = per_cycle_rms_and_delta_filtered(sig, P, rel_floor=1e-6, max_cycles=4096)
        bar_delta = float(np.mean(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
        std_delta = float(np.std(deltas)) if len(deltas)>0 else 0.0
        Qpsi = (math.pi/bar_delta) if bar_delta>0 else None
        rows.append((stride, P, len(deltas), bar_delta, std_delta, (float(Qpsi) if Qpsi and np.isfinite(Qpsi) else None)))
    return rows

rows = ledger_stride("SHA-CHAIN", SHA_CHAIN, 2, 16)
print("stride, P, nΔ, δ̄, std, Qψ")
for r in rows:
    sd, P, nD, bd, sdv, Q = r
    Qs = "None" if Q is None else f"{Q:.3f}"
    print(sd, P, nD, f"{bd:.6f}", f"{sdv:.6f}", Qs)
